<script>		
			/****************************************************************************************************************************/
			/*	JW PLATFORM v7: 																										*/
			/****************************************************************************************************************************/
			function PintarVideoJW(video_id, div_id, video_mosca, thumb_width, ProporcionAncho, ProporcionAlto, tagpubli){
				/*Version 7 de JWPlatform*/
				$('#ThumbNail_'+div_id).hide();
				$('#Video_'+div_id).show();

				var timeStamp	= new Date().getTime();

				var urlParts 	= window.location.href.split('/');
				var url 	 	= urlParts[0] + '//' + urlParts[2];
				var domain		= urlParts[2];
				
				tagpubli		= trim(tagpubli);

				var ads_id = '';

				switch(domain){case"www.elperiodicodearagon.com":case"www.elperiodicodearagon.c":ads_id="epa";break;case"www.lacronicabadajoz.com":case"www.lacronicabadajoz.c":ads_id="cba";break;case"www.diariocordoba.com":case"www.diariocordoba.c":ads_id="dc";break;case"www.elperiodicomediterraneo.com":case"www.elperiodicomediterraneo.c":ads_id="epm";break;case"www.elperiodicoextremadura.com":case"www.elperiodicoextremadura.c":ads_id="ext";break;case"pt.elperiodicoextremadura.com":case"pt.elperiodicoextremadura.c":ads_id="ext";break;case"www.diariolagrada.com":case"www.diariolagrada.c":ads_id="lg";break;case"www.elcorreodeburgos.com":case"www.elcorreodeburgos.c":ads_id="cb";break;case"www.heraldodiariodesoria.es":case"www.heraldodiariodesoria.c":ads_id="ds";break;case"www.diariodevalladolid.es":case"www.diariodevalladolid.c":ads_id="dv";break;case"www.elperiodicodelazulejo.es":case"www.elperiodicodelazulejo.c":ads_id="azu"}

				/*GOOGLE DFP EPI - PREROLL*/
				var ads_tag	= 'https://pubads.g.doubleclick.net/gampad/ads';
				ads_tag += '?sz=640x360&iu=/138855687/nacional-' + ads_id + '/' + ads_id + '-generico_vc&ciu_szs=1x1,728x90,970x90,980x90,970x250,980x250,620x72,300x250,300x600,980x45,320x50,320x100&impl=s&cust_params=p%3Dcpm_preroll%26t%3Dtema%26&gdfp_req=1&env=vp&ad_rule=1&output=vmap&unviewed_position_start=1&url=' + encodeURI(url) + '&description_url=' + encodeURI(url) + '&correlator=' + timeStamp;

				console.log ('*** EPI - PREROLL *** '+ads_tag+' ******');
				
				/*CREACION*/
				var playerInstance 	= jwplayer('Video_' + div_id);

				/*OPCIONES DEL PLAYER*/
				var playerOptions = {
					file: 		"https://content.jwplatform.com/videos/" + video_id + ".mp4",
					image: 		"https://content.jwplatform.com/thumbs/" + video_id + "-" + thumb_width + ".jpg",
					aspectratio: ProporcionAncho + ":" + ProporcionAlto,
					logo: 		{file: video_mosca, link: url},
					abouttext:	url,
					aboutlink: 	url,
					primary: 	"html5", //flash
					controls: 	true,
					autostart : true,
					ga: {} 		//google analytics
				};
				
				if(ads_id.length>0){
					playerOptions.advertising = {
						client: 'googima'
						, tag: ads_tag
						, skipoffset: 11
						, skipmessage: "Saltar anuncio en xx segundos"
						, skiptext: "Ir a video &gt;"
						, admessage: "El anuncio terminara en xx s."
						, cuetext: "PULSE PARA VISITAR ANUNCIANTE"
					};
				}

				/*EJECUTAR*/
				playerInstance.setup(playerOptions);

				/*EVENTOS*/
				playerInstance.on('ready', function(e) {
					var AnchoVideo= $('#Video_'+div_id).innerWidth();
					var AltoVideo= $('#Video_'+div_id).innerHeight();
					if ($('#ThumbNail_'+div_id).parents('.bx-viewport').length) {
						$('#ThumbNail_'+div_id).parents('.bx-viewport').css({
							width: AnchoVideo,
							height: AltoVideo
						});
					}
				});
			}
		</script>

			      __cmp("getGooglePersonalization", function(consent, isSuccess) {
			          if (isSuccess && consent.googlePersonalizationData.consentValue) {
			              googletag.pubads().setRequestNonPersonalizedAds(0);
			          } else {
			              googletag.pubads().setRequestNonPersonalizedAds(1);
			          }
			          advertisingStatus.ready = true;
			          if (advertisingStatus.pending.length) {
			              googletag.pubads().refresh(advertisingStatus.pending);
			          }
			          advertisingStatus.pending = [];
			      });
			});
			var advertisingShow = function(id, slot) {
			      googletag.cmd.push(function() {
			          if (slot) {
			              googletag.display(id);
			              if (advertisingStatus.ready) {
			                  googletag.pubads().refresh([slot]);
			              } else {
			                  advertisingStatus.pending.push(slot);
			              }
			          }
			      });
			};
		</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
				function frm_checkLoginModal(){
					var login_Email = get_id('login_EmailModal');
					login_Email.value = trim(login_Email.value);
					if( login_Email.value=='' ){
						alert('Atención:\nPara acceder es necesario que nos indiques el email con el que te registraste.');
						login_Email.focus();
						return false;
					}
					if( !VerificarMail(login_Email.value) ){
						alert('Atención:\nRevisa tu email, no es correcto.');
						login_Email.focus();
						return false;
					}
					var login_Password = get_id('login_PasswordModal');
					login_Password.value = trim(login_Password.value);
					if( login_Password.value=='' ){
						alert('Atención:\nPara continuar es necesario que nos indiques la contraseña asociada a tu cuenta.');
						login_Password.focus();
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
			</script>
